HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001328/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0041894be8d16b6855af","text":"4. **Quiet blocks the vessel sequence from becoming noisy entertainment.** **Lexical:** `ك و ب` B002, the `كوبة` as a music or play instrument, activated alongside B001 cup. **Cue:** 88:11 has categorically removed vain or noisy speech before cups appear. **Role:** the instrument branch opens an auditory convivial possibility, while the established silence constrains its mechanism to quiet material hospitality. **Abduction:** I infer that the earlier acoustic rule governs later objects. **Reading change:** the vessel image can carry festivity, but here festivity is reordered around drink and repose rather than clamor.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001328/B005","root_001657/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_38b10441860e2813fa40","text":"3. **No coercive drinking.** Evidence: `س ق ي` B001 forced/passive being given drink in 88:5; `ك و ب` B005 drinking from a cup; `و ض ع` B010 deposit/entrusting. Structural cue: punishment drink is administered; garden cups are simply placed. Role: the cup allows non-coercive availability. Abductive move: the same water economy distinguishes compulsion from entrusted hospitality. Reading change: the ayah quietly reverses `تسقى`.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001328/B001","root_001328/B005","root_001657/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4d2c7f00eeae6042e7c3","text":"1. **Placed cups complete a source-to-user delivery path.** **Lexical:** `ك و ب` B001, \"cup without a handle,\" and B005, drinking from a cup; `و ض ع` B001, setting something down or in its place. **Cue:** the running spring of 88:12 is followed by couches in 88:13 and then cups deliberately set in 88:14. **Role:** the spring supplies water, the cup converts flow into a drinkable portion, and placement locates that portion beside a resting user. **Abduction:** I infer filling and service from adjacency; neither contents nor server is stated. **Reading change:** the ayah becomes the terminal stage of a provisioning chain, where environmental abundance is translated into individual access.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001328/B001","root_001657/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_593188c29a0e4287938e","text":"2. **Lower placement is a form of accessibility, not loss of rank.** **Lexical:** `و ض ع` B001, deliberate placement, contrasted with B005, \"lowness of rank and humility\"; `ك و ب` B001 specifies a handleless vessel. **Cue:** the cups come after a high garden and raised couches, while mountains are set upright in 88:19. **Role:** deliberate lowering positions fragile, handleless vessels for use inside an elevated setting; the rank branch supplies a possible low/high tension without making the cups degraded. **Abduction:** I infer ergonomic placement from the cup's form and surrounding heights. **Reading change:** `موضوعة` reads as calibrated service: honor does not require every object to be high, only each to be at its useful level.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001657/B013"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6941f40d303159588856","text":"3. **The camel retrospectively activates lowering as assistance.** **Lexical:** `و ض ع` B013, \"lowering a camel's head or neck so a rider can mount.\" **Cue:** the cups' placement is followed three ayat later by the invitation to inspect the camel in 88:17. **Role:** B013 supplies a concrete model in which downward movement enables another's access; that model transfers functionally to the placed cup. **Abduction:** I infer analogy rather than shared literal action: no camel lowers the cups. **Reading change:** placement is newly visible as hospitable lowering, an object's position adjusted to the needs of its user.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001657/B013"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6ddae005466187fc8ac6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:14:2\",\"root_001657\",[\"B013\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Daha sonraki deve, binicinin erişmesi için boynunu indirme görüntüsünü canlandırarak kabın konumunu kullanıcının ihtiyacına göre yapılan konuksever bir alçaltma gibi gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001657/B001","root_001657/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_70d6bce0e8fd3f52bf2d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:14:2\",\"root_001657\",[\"B001\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Aşağı koyma, yüksek bahçede bir değer kaybı değil, kulpsuz kabı kullanıcının erişimine göre ayarlayan hizmet biçimidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001328/B001","root_001657/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_88ceba8784a9cd982bd2","text":"1. **Cups placed in ready service.** Evidence: `ك و ب` B001 cup/goblet, B005 drinking from a cup; `و ض ع` B001 setting something down/in its place. Structural cue: the running spring is followed by drink-vessels set out. Role: the cups mediate the spring into hospitable access. Abductive move: provision becomes mercy when it is placed within reach.
